codex2html

Transcript-style rendering of a Codex rollout file.

User
hello, i would like to have a script that turns a codex session ('the rollout-files') into a web page. the web page should basically look like the 'transcript' mode you can enter in codex. Ideally, there should be four distinct types of elements, perhaps color-coded: 1) the prompts given by the user 2) the replies given by codex 3) the 'thinking' output done by codex and 4) the code output printed in codex. The 'thinking' and the code output should be minimised by default
Thinking 
I’m going to inspect the session workspace to find the rollout file format and any existing tooling, then I’ll build the converter and verify it against the available data.

Code Output (apply_patch) 
*** Begin Patch
*** Add File: /var/www/html/dev/codex-sessions/rollout_to_html.py
+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""
+Convert Codex rollout/session files into a standalone transcript-style HTML page.
+
+The renderer groups content into four visual classes:
+1. user prompts
+2. assistant replies
+3. assistant thinking / progress output
+4. code / tool / command output
+
+Thinking and code sections are collapsed by default.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import html
+import json
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any
+
+
+TEXT_KEYS = (
+    "text",
+    "content",
+    "message",
+    "output",
+    "value",
+    "body",
+    "stdout",
+    "stderr",
+    "input",
+)
+
+
+def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
+        description="Render a Codex rollout/session file as a transcript-style HTML page."
+    )
+    parser.add_argument("input", type=Path, help="Path to the rollout/session file")
+    parser.add_argument(
+        "-o",
+        "--output",
+        type=Path,
+        help="Output HTML path (defaults to <input>.html)",
+    )
+    parser.add_argument(
+        "--title",
+        help="Optional page title override",
+    )
+    return parser.parse_args()
+
+
+def load_input(path: Path) -> Any:
+    raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+    stripped = raw.strip()
+    if not stripped:
+        return []
+
+    try:
+        return json.loads(stripped)
+    except json.JSONDecodeError:
+        pass
+
+    records: list[Any] = []
+    jsonl_ok = True
+    for line in raw.splitlines():
+        if not line.strip():
+            continue
+        try:
+            records.append(json.loads(line))
+        except json.JSONDecodeError:
+            jsonl_ok = False
+            break
+
+    if jsonl_ok and records:
+        return records
+
+    return {"role": "assistant", "channel": "final", "content": raw}
+
+
+def iter_message_candidates(obj: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
+    found: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
+
+    def visit(node: Any) -> None:
+        if isinstance(node, dict):
+            if any(key in node for key in ("role", "channel", "type", "content", "message", "output")):
+                found.append(node)
+            for value in node.values():
+                visit(value)
+        elif isinstance(node, list):
+            for item in node:
+                visit(item)
+
+    visit(obj)
+    return found
+
+
+def flatten_text(value: Any) -> str:
+    if value is None:
+        return ""
+    if isinstance(value, str):
+        return value
+    if isinstance(value, (int, float, bool)):
+        return str(value)
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        parts = [flatten_text(item) for item in value]
+        return "\n".join(part for part in parts if part.strip())
+    if isinstance(value, dict):
+        if value.get("type") in {"input_text", "output_text", "text", "reasoning", "summary"}:
+            return flatten_text(value.get("text") or value.get("content"))
+        for key in TEXT_KEYS:
+            if key in value:
+                text = flatten_text(value[key])
+                if text.strip():
+                    return text
+        if "args" in value:
+            return flatten_text(value["args"])
+        return json.dumps(value, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
+    return str(value)
+
+
+def classify_message(item: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
+    role = str(item.get("role", "")).lower()
+    channel = str(item.get("channel", "")).lower()
+    msg_type = str(item.get("type", "")).lower()
+    name = str(item.get("name", "")).lower()
+
+    if role == "user":
+        return "user"
+
+    if role in {"tool", "function"}:
+        return "code"
+
+    if role == "assistant":
+        if channel == "final":
+            return "assistant"
+        if channel in {"analysis", "commentary", "summary"}:
+            return "thinking"
+
+    if msg_type in {"tool_result", "tool_call", "function_call", "function_result", "command"}:
+        return "code"
+    if msg_type in {"reasoning", "analysis", "thought", "commentary"}:
+        return "thinking"
+    if msg_type in {"message", "assistant_message"} and role == "assistant":
+        return "assistant"
+
+    if channel in {"analysis", "commentary"}:
+        return "thinking"
+    if channel == "final":
+        return "assistant"
+
+    if name in {"exec_command", "write_stdin", "apply_patch"}:
+        return "code"
+
+    text = flatten_text(item).strip()
+    if not text:
+        return None
+
+    if role == "assistant":
+        return "assistant"
+
+    return None
+
+
+def extract_label(item: dict[str, Any], kind: str) -> str:
+    if kind == "user":
+        return "User"
+    if kind == "assistant":
+        return "Codex"
+    if kind == "thinking":
+        channel = str(item.get("channel", "")).lower()
+        if channel:
+            return f"Thinking ({channel})"
+        return "Thinking"
+    if kind == "code":
+        msg_type = item.get("type") or item.get("name") or item.get("channel")
+        if msg_type:
+            return f"Code Output ({msg_type})"
+        return "Code Output"
+    return "Message"
+
+
+def normalize_messages(data: Any) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
+    raw_items: list[dict[str, Any]]
+    if isinstance(data, list):
+        raw_items = [item for item in data if isinstance(item, dict)]
+    elif isinstance(data, dict) and isinstance(data.get("messages"), list):
+        raw_items = [item for item in data["messages"] if isinstance(item, dict)]
+    elif isinstance(data, dict) and isinstance(data.get("items"), list):
+        raw_items = [item for item in data["items"] if isinstance(item, dict)]
+    else:
+        raw_items = iter_message_candidates(data)
+
+    normalized: list[dict[str, str]] = []
+    for item in raw_items:
+        kind = classify_message(item)
+        if not kind:
+            continue
+
+        text = (
+            flatten_text(item.get("content"))
+            or flatten_text(item.get("message"))
+            or flatten_text(item.get("output"))
+            or flatten_text(item)
+        ).strip()
+        if not text:
+            continue
+
+        normalized.append(
+            {
+                "kind": kind,
+                "label": extract_label(item, kind),
+                "text": text,
+            }
+        )
+
+    compacted: list[dict[str, str]] = []
+    for entry in normalized:
+        if compacted and compacted[-1]["kind"] == entry["kind"] and compacted[-1]["label"] == entry["label"]:
+            compacted[-1]["text"] += "\n\n" + entry["text"]
+        else:
+            compacted.append(entry)
+    return compacted
+
+
+def render_message(entry: dict[str, str]) -> str:
+    kind = entry["kind"]
+    label = html.escape(entry["label"])
+    body = html.escape(entry["text"])
+    collapsible = kind in {"thinking", "code"}
+
+    if collapsible:
+        return f"""
+        <details class="message {kind}">
+          <summary><span class="label">{label}</span></summary>
+          <pre>{body}</pre>
+        </details>
+        """.strip()
+
+    return f"""
+    <section class="message {kind}">
+      <div class="label">{label}</div>
+      <pre>{body}</pre>
+    </section>
+    """.strip()
+
+
+def build_html(title: str, messages: list[dict[str, str]]) -> str:
+    body = "\n".join(render_message(message) for message in messages)
+    return f"""<!DOCTYPE html>
+<html lang="en">
+<head>
+  <meta charset="utf-8">
+  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
+  <title>{html.escape(title)}</title>
+  <style>
+    :root {{
+      --bg: #f5f1e8;
+      --panel: #fffdf8;
+      --border: #d8cdbd;
+      --text: #1e1a16;
+      --muted: #6b6259;
+      --user: #dbeafe;
+      --assistant: #e7f7df;
+      --thinking: #fef3c7;
+      --code: #ece7ff;
+      --shadow: 0 12px 30px rgba(56, 43, 27, 0.08);
+    }}
+    * {{ box-sizing: border-box; }}
+    body {{
+      margin: 0;
+      font-family: "IBM Plex Sans", "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
+      color: var(--text);
+      background:
+        radial-gradient(circle at top left, rgba(255,255,255,0.75), transparent 30%),
+        linear-gradient(180deg, #f3efe5 0%, #ede7db 100%);
+    }}
+    .page {{
+      max-width: 980px;
+      margin: 0 auto;
+      padding: 32px 16px 48px;
+    }}
+    .header {{
+      margin-bottom: 20px;
+      padding: 20px 22px;
+      background: rgba(255, 253, 248, 0.8);
+      border: 1px solid var(--border);
+      border-radius: 18px;
+      box-shadow: var(--shadow);
+      backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
+    }}
+    h1 {{
+      margin: 0 0 8px;
+      font-family: "IBM Plex Serif", Georgia, serif;
+      font-size: clamp(1.6rem, 3vw, 2.3rem);
+    }}
+    .subtle {{
+      margin: 0;
+      color: var(--muted);
+    }}
+    .toolbar {{
+      display: flex;
+      gap: 10px;
+      flex-wrap: wrap;
+      margin: 18px 0 24px;
+    }}
+    button {{
+      border: 1px solid var(--border);
+      background: var(--panel);
+      color: var(--text);
+      padding: 10px 14px;
+      border-radius: 999px;
+      cursor: pointer;
+      font: inherit;
+      box-shadow: var(--shadow);
+    }}
+    .transcript {{
+      display: grid;
+      gap: 14px;
+    }}
+    .message {{
+      border: 1px solid var(--border);
+      border-radius: 18px;
+      background: var(--panel);
+      overflow: hidden;
+      box-shadow: var(--shadow);
+    }}
+    .message.user {{ border-left: 8px solid #60a5fa; background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--user), #f8fbff); }}
+    .message.assistant {{ border-left: 8px solid #65a30d; background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--assistant), #fbfff8); }}
+    .message.thinking {{ border-left: 8px solid #d97706; background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--thinking), #fffdf4); }}
+    .message.code {{ border-left: 8px solid #7c3aed; background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--code), #faf8ff); }}
+    .label {{
+      display: block;
+      padding: 14px 18px 0;
+      font-weight: 700;
+      letter-spacing: 0.02em;
+    }}
+    .message pre {{
+      margin: 0;
+      padding: 12px 18px 18px;
+      white-space: pre-wrap;
+      word-break: break-word;
+      font-family: "IBM Plex Mono", "SFMono-Regular", monospace;
+      font-size: 0.95rem;
+      line-height: 1.5;
+    }}
+    details > summary {{
+      list-style: none;
+      cursor: pointer;
+      padding: 0 0 14px;
+    }}
+    details > summary::-webkit-details-marker {{ display: none; }}
+    details > summary .label::after {{
+      content: " (collapsed)";
+      color: var(--muted);
+      font-weight: 500;
+    }}
+    details[open] > summary .label::after {{
+      content: " (expanded)";
+    }}
+    @media (max-width: 640px) {{
+      .page {{ padding: 20px 10px 36px; }}
+      .header {{ padding: 16px; }}
+      .message pre {{ font-size: 0.9rem; }}
+    }}
+  </style>
+</head>
+<body>
+  <main class="page">
+    <header class="header">
+      <h1>{html.escape(title)}</h1>
+      <p class="subtle">Transcript-style rendering of a Codex rollout file.</p>
+    </header>
+    <div class="toolbar">
+      <button type="button" data-target="thinking">Toggle Thinking</button>
+      <button type="button" data-target="code">Toggle Code Output</button>
+    </div>
+    <section class="transcript">
+      {body or '<section class="message assistant"><div class="label">Codex</div><pre>No transcript content was detected.</pre></section>'}
+    </section>
+  </main>
+  <script>
+    document.querySelectorAll('.toolbar button').forEach((button) => {{
+      button.addEventListener('click', () => {{
+        const kind = button.dataset.target;
+        document.querySelectorAll(`details.${{kind}}`).forEach((node) => {{
+          node.open = !node.open;
+        }});
+      }});
+    }});
+  </script>
+</body>
+</html>
+"""
+
+
+def main() -> int:
+    args = parse_args()
+    source = args.input
+    output = args.output or source.with_suffix(source.suffix + ".html")
+    title = args.title or f"Codex Transcript: {source.name}"
+
+    data = load_input(source)
+    messages = normalize_messages(data)
+    rendered = build_html(title, messages)
+
+    output.write_text(rendered, encoding="utf-8")
+    print(f"Wrote {output}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())
*** End Patch

    82	def iter_message_candidates(obj: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:

    99	def flatten_text(value: Any) -> str:
   100	    if value is None:
   101	        return ""
   102	    if isinstance(value, str):
   103	        return value
   104	    if isinstance(value, (int, float, bool)):
   105	        return str(value)
   106	    if isinstance(value, list):
   107	        parts = [flatten_text(item) for item in value]
   108	        return "\n".join(part for part in parts if part.strip())
   109	    if isinstance(value, dict):
   110	        if value.get("type") in {"input_text", "output_text", "text", "reasoning", "summary"}:
   111	            return flatten_text(value.get("text") or value.get("content"))
   112	        for key in TEXT_KEYS:

   115	                if text.strip():

   117	        if "args" in value:
   118	            return flatten_text(value["args"])
   119	        return json.dumps(value, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
   120	    return str(value)

   123	def classify_message(item: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
   124	    role = str(item.get("role", "")).lower()
   125	    channel = str(item.get("channel", "")).lower()
   126	    msg_type = str(item.get("type", "")).lower()
   127	    name = str(item.get("name", "")).lower()
   128	
   129	    if role == "user":
   130	        return "user"
   131	
   132	    if role in {"tool", "function"}:
   133	        return "code"
   134	
   135	    if role == "assistant":

   137	            return "assistant"
   138	        if channel in {"analysis", "commentary", "summary"}:

   140

   147	
   148	    if channel in {"analysis", "commentary"}:
   149	        return "thinking"
   150	    if channel == "final":
   151	        return "assistant"
   152	
   153	    if name in {"exec_command", "write_stdin", "apply_patch"}:
   154	        return "code"

   158	        return None

   161	        return "assistant"

   163	    return None

   165	
   166	def extract_label(item: dict[str, Any], kind: str) -> str:
   167	    if kind == "user":
   168	        return "User"
   169	    if kind == "assistant":
   170	        return "Codex"
   171	    if kind == "thinking":
   172	        channel = str(item.get("channel", "")).lower()
   173	        if channel:
   174	            return f"Thinking ({channel})"

   176	    if kind == "code":

   178	        if msg_type:
   179	            return f"Code Output ({msg_type})"

   184	def normalize_messages(data: Any) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
   185	    raw_items: list[dict[str, Any]]
   186	    if isinstance(data, list):
   187	        raw_items = [item for item in data if isinstance(item, dict)]
   188	    elif isinstance(data, dict) and isinstance(data.get("messages"), list):
   189	        raw_items = [item for item in data["messages"] if isinstance(item, dict)]
   190	    elif isinstance(data, dict) and isinstance(data.get("items"), list):
   191	        raw_items = [item for item in data["items"] if isinstance(item, dict)]
   192	    else:
   193	        raw_items = iter_message_candidates(data)
   194	
   195	    normalized: list[dict[str, str]] = []
   196	    for item in raw_items:
   197	        kind = classify_message(item)
   198	        if not kind:
   199	            continue
   200	
   201	        text = (
   202	            flatten_text(item.get("content"))
   203	            or flatten_text(item.get("message"))
   204	            or flatten_text(item.get("output"))
   205	            or flatten_text(item)
   206	        ).strip()

   208	            continue
   209	
   210	        normalized.append(
   211	            {
   212	                "kind": kind,
   213	                "label": extract_label(item, kind),
   214	                "text": text,
   215	            }
   216	        )
   217	
   218	    compacted: list[dict[str, str]] = []
   219	    for entry in normalized:
   220	        if compacted and compacted[-1]["kind"] == entry["kind"] and compacted[-1]["label"] == entry["label"]:
   221	            compacted[-1]["text"] += "\n\n" + entry["text"]
   222	        else:
   223	            compacted.append(entry)
   224	    return compacted
   225	
   226	
   227	def render_message(entry: dict[str, str]) -> str:
   228	    kind = entry["kind"]
   229	    label = html.escape(entry["label"])
   230	    body = html.escape(entry["text"])
   231	    collapsible = kind in {"thinking", "code"}
   232	
   233	    if collapsible:
   234	        return f"""
   235	        <details class="message {kind}">
   236	          <summary><span class="label">{label}</span></summary>
   237	          <pre>{body}</pre>
   238	        </details>
   239	        """.strip()
   240	
   241	    return f"""
   242	    <section class="message {kind}">
   243	      <div class="label">{label}</div>
   244	      <pre>{body}</pre>
   245	    </section>
   246	    """.strip()
   247	
   248	
   249	def build_html(title: str, messages: list[dict[str, str]]) -> str:
   250	    body = "\n".join(render_message(message) for message in messages)
   251	    return f"""<!DOCTYPE html>
   252	<html lang="en">
   253	<head>
   254	  <meta charset="utf-8">
   255	  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">

   257	  <style>

Codex
Created a standalone Python converter at [rollout_to_html.py](/var/www/html/dev/codex-sessions/rollout_to_html.py). It reads a rollout/session file, tries JSON first, falls back to JSONL, then renders a transcript-style HTML page with four visual classes: user, Codex reply, thinking, and code output. Thinking and code sections are collapsed by default using `<details>`.

You can run it like this:

```bash
python3 /var/www/html/dev/codex-sessions/rollout_to_html.py /path/to/rollout.json
```

Optional output/title overrides are supported:

```bash
python3 /var/www/html/dev/codex-sessions/rollout_to_html.py rollout.json -o transcript.html --title "My Codex Session"
```

I also added a small example input at [sample_rollout.json](/var/www/html/dev/codex-sessions/sample_rollout.json) and verified the script compiles and generates HTML successfully. One important caveat: I did not have a real Codex rollout file in this workspace, so the parser is intentionally tolerant rather than tied to one exact schema. If you drop in an actual rollout file and its structure differs, I can tighten the parser against the real format.

Thinking 
I’ve identified the problem: your JSONL includes both transcript events and a lot of metadata/duplication, and the first version was treating the wrong layer as the conversation. I’m rewriting extraction to target the real event stream and to skip instruction noise, token counters, and duplicate copies.

@@
 def load_input(path: Path) -> Any:
@@
     return {"role": "assistant", "channel": "final", "content": raw}
+
+
+def parse_json_maybe(text: str) -> Any:
+    try:
+        return json.loads(text)
+    except Exception:
+        return None
@@
 def flatten_text(value: Any) -> str:
@@
         if "args" in value:
             return flatten_text(value["args"])
+        if "arguments" in value:
+            return flatten_text(value["arguments"])
         return json.dumps(value, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
     return str(value)
+
+
+def should_skip_instruction_text(text: str) -> bool:
+    stripped = text.strip()
+    if not stripped:
+        return True
+    prefixes = (
+        "<permissions instructions>",
+        "<collaboration_mode>",
+        "# AGENTS.md instructions",
+    )
+    return stripped.startswith(prefixes)
+
+
+def format_tool_call(name: str, arguments: str) -> str:
+    args_obj = parse_json_maybe(arguments)
+    if isinstance(args_obj, dict):
+        interesting = []
+        for key in ("cmd", "workdir", "justification", "shell", "yield_time_ms"):
+            if key in args_obj:
+                interesting.append(f"{key}: {args_obj[key]}")
+        if interesting:
+            return f"{name}\n" + "\n".join(interesting)
+    return f"{name}\n{arguments}".strip()
+
+
+def extract_from_jsonl_records(records: list[Any]) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
+    messages: list[dict[str, str]] = []
+
+    for record in records:
+        if not isinstance(record, dict):
+            continue
+
+        top_type = record.get("type")
+        payload = record.get("payload")
+        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
+            continue
+
+        payload_type = payload.get("type")
+
+        if top_type == "event_msg" and payload_type == "user_message":
+            text = flatten_text(payload.get("message")).strip()
+            if text and not should_skip_instruction_text(text):
+                messages.append({"kind": "user", "label": "User", "text": text})
+            continue
+
+        if top_type == "event_msg" and payload_type == "agent_message":
+            text = flatten_text(payload.get("message")).strip()
+            if not text:
+                continue
+            phase = str(payload.get("phase", "")).lower()
+            kind = "assistant" if phase == "final_answer" else "thinking"
+            label = "Codex" if kind == "assistant" else "Thinking"
+            messages.append({"kind": kind, "label": label, "text": text})
+            continue
+
+        if top_type == "event_msg" and payload_type == "agent_reasoning":
+            text = flatten_text(payload.get("text")).strip()
+            if text:
+                messages.append({"kind": "thinking", "label": "Thinking", "text": text})
+            continue
+
+        if top_type == "response_item" and payload_type == "function_call":
+            name = str(payload.get("name", "tool"))
+            arguments = flatten_text(payload.get("arguments")).strip()
+            text = format_tool_call(name, arguments)
+            messages.append({"kind": "code", "label": f"Code Output ({name})", "text": text})
+            continue
+
+        if top_type == "response_item" and payload_type == "function_call_output":
+            call_id = str(payload.get("call_id", "")).strip()
+            output = flatten_text(payload.get("output")).strip()
+            if output:
+                label = f"Code Output ({call_id})" if call_id else "Code Output"
+                messages.append({"kind": "code", "label": label, "text": output})
+            continue
+
+        if top_type == "response_item" and payload_type == "custom_tool_call":
+            name = str(payload.get("name", "tool"))
+            tool_input = flatten_text(payload.get("input")).strip()
+            if tool_input:
+                messages.append({"kind": "code", "label": f"Code Output ({name})", "text": tool_input})
+            continue
+
+        if top_type == "response_item" and payload_type == "custom_tool_call_output":
+            output = flatten_text(payload.get("output")).strip()
+            parsed = parse_json_maybe(output)
+            if isinstance(parsed, dict) and "output" in parsed:
+                output = flatten_text(parsed.get("output")).strip()
+            if output:
+                messages.append({"kind": "code", "label": "Code Output", "text": output})
+            continue
+
+    compacted: list[dict[str, str]] = []
+    for entry in messages:
+        if compacted and compacted[-1]["kind"] == entry["kind"] and compacted[-1]["label"] == entry["label"]:
+            compacted[-1]["text"] += "\n\n" + entry["text"]
+        else:
+            compacted.append(entry)
+    return compacted
@@
 def normalize_messages(data: Any) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
+    if isinstance(data, list) and data and all(isinstance(item, dict) and "timestamp" in item and "type" in item for item in data):
+        extracted = extract_from_jsonl_records(data)
+        if extracted:
+            return extracted
+
     raw_items: list[dict[str, Any]]
*** End Patch

import json
from collections import Counter
c=Counter(); pc=Counter(); phases=Counter(); roles=Counter()
with open('test2.jsonl','r',encoding='utf-8') as f:
    for line in f:
        if not line.strip():
            continue
        obj=json.loads(line)
        c[obj.get('type')] += 1
        p=obj.get('payload')
        if isinstance(p, dict):
            pc[p.get('type')] += 1
            if 'phase' in p: phases[p.get('phase')] += 1
            if 'role' in p: roles[p.get('role')] += 1
print('top', c)
print('payload', pc)
print('phases', phases)
print('roles', roles)

Codex
Yes. `test2.jsonl` is using an older session layout.

The key difference is:
- `test.jsonl` had many final Codex replies in `event_msg -> agent_message` with `phase: final_answer`
- `test2.jsonl` stores final Codex replies mainly in `response_item -> message` with `role: assistant` and `phase: final_answer`
- it also includes an unlabeled `event_msg -> agent_message` copy of some completion text, which made the older parser misclassify parts as thinking

I updated [rollout_to_html.py](/var/www/html/dev/codex-sessions/rollout_to_html.py) to support both formats, and regenerated [test2.html](/var/www/html/dev/codex-sessions/test2.html). The Codex final replies are now present there.

So the answer is: yes, `test2.jsonl` is effectively another markup/version of the rollout export. The converter now handles both `test.jsonl` and `test2.jsonl`.

def main() -> int:
    args = parse_args()
    source = args.input
    output = args.output or source.with_suffix(source.suffix + ".html")
    title = args.title or f"Codex Transcript: {source.name}"

    data = load_input(source)
    messages = normalize_messages(data)
    rendered = build_html(title, messages)

    output.write_text(rendered, encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"Wrote {output}")
    return 0

    const viewButtons = document.querySelectorAll('[data-view]');
    const allMessages = document.querySelectorAll('.message');
    let activeView = 'all';

    function applyView(nextView) {{
      activeView = activeView === nextView ? 'all' : nextView;
      allMessages.forEach((node) => {{
        const hideForConversation = activeView === 'conversation' &&
          (node.classList.contains('thinking') || node.classList.contains('code'));
        const hideForUser = activeView === 'user' && !node.classList.contains('user');
        node.classList.toggle('is-hidden', hideForConversation || hideForUser);
      }});
      viewButtons.forEach((viewButton) => {{
        viewButton.classList.toggle('is-active', viewButton.dataset.view === activeView);
      }});
    }}

    document.querySelectorAll('.toolbar button').forEach((button) => {{
      button.addEventListener('click', () => {{
        if (button.dataset.target) {{
          const kind = button.dataset.target;
          document.querySelectorAll(`details.${{kind}}`).forEach((node) => {{
            node.open = !node.open;
          }});
          return;
        }}

        if (button.dataset.view) {{
          applyView(button.dataset.view);
        }}
      }});
    }});

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Convert Codex rollout/session files into a standalone transcript-style HTML page.

The renderer groups content into four visual classes:
1. user prompts
2. assistant replies
3. assistant thinking / progress output
4. code / tool / command output

Thinking and code sections are collapsed by default.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import html
import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any


TEXT_KEYS = (
    "text",
    "content",
    "message",
    "output",
    "value",
    "body",
    "stdout",
    "stderr",
    "input",
)


def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Render a Codex rollout/session file as a transcript-style HTML page."
    )
    parser.add_argument("input", type=Path, help="Path to the rollout/session file")
    parser.add_argument(
        "-o",
        "--output",
        type=Path,
        help="Output HTML path (defaults to <input>.html)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--title",
        help="Optional page title override",
    )
    return parser.parse_args()


def load_input(path: Path) -> Any:
    raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    stripped = raw.strip()
    if not stripped:
        return []

    try:
        return json.loads(stripped)
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        pass

    records: list[Any] = []
    jsonl_ok = True
    for line in raw.splitlines():
        if not line.strip():
            continue
        try:
            records.append(json.loads(line))
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            jsonl_ok = False
            break

    if jsonl_ok and records:
        return records

    return {"role": "assistant", "channel": "final", "content": raw}


def parse_json_maybe(text: str) -> Any:
    try:
        return json.loads(text)
    except Exception:
        return None


def iter_message_candidates(obj: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    found: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    def visit(node: Any) -> None:
        if isinstance(node, dict):
            if any(key in node for key in ("role", "channel", "type", "content", "message", "output")):
                found.append(node)
            for value in node.values():
                visit(value)
        elif isinstance(node, list):
            for item in node:
                visit(item)

    visit(obj)
    return found


def flatten_text(value: Any) -> str:
    if value is None:
        return ""
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, (int, float, bool)):
        return str(value)
    if isinstance(value, list):
        parts = [flatten_text(item) for item in value]
        return "\n".join(part for part in parts if part.strip())
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        if value.get("type") in {"input_text", "output_text", "text", "reasoning", "summary"}:
            return flatten_text(value.get("text") or value.get("content"))
        for key in TEXT_KEYS:
            if key in value:
                text = flatten_text(value[key])
                if text.strip():
                    return text
        if "args" in value:
            return flatten_text(value["args"])
        if "arguments" in value:
            return flatten_text(value["arguments"])
        return json.dumps(value, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
    return str(value)


def should_skip_instruction_text(text: str) -> bool:
    stripped = text.strip()
    if not stripped:
        return True
    prefixes = (
        "<permissions instructions>",
        "<collaboration_mode>",
        "# AGENTS.md instructions",
    )
    return stripped.startswith(prefixes)


def format_tool_call(name: str, arguments: str) -> str:
    args_obj = parse_json_maybe(arguments)
    if isinstance(args_obj, dict):
        interesting = []
        for key in ("cmd", "workdir", "justification", "shell", "yield_time_ms"):
            if key in args_obj:
                interesting.append(f"{key}: {args_obj[key]}")
        if interesting:
            return f"{name}\n" + "\n".join(interesting)
    return f"{name}\n{arguments}".strip()


def looks_like_final_assistant_message(text: str) -> bool:
    stripped = text.strip()
    if not stripped:
        return False
    starters = (
        "implemented",
        "added",
        "updated",
        "fixed",
        "created",
        "completed",
        "changed",
    )
    lowered = stripped.lower()
    return lowered.startswith(starters) or "validation:" in lowered or "what changed" in lowered


def extract_from_jsonl_records(records: list[Any]) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    messages: list[dict[str, str]] = []

    for record in records:
        if not isinstance(record, dict):
            continue

        top_type = record.get("type")
        payload = record.get("payload")
        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
            continue

        payload_type = payload.get("type")

        if top_type == "event_msg" and payload_type == "user_message":
            text = flatten_text(payload.get("message")).strip()
            if text and not should_skip_instruction_text(text):
                messages.append({"kind": "user", "label": "User", "text": text})
            continue

        if top_type == "event_msg" and payload_type == "agent_message":
            text = flatten_text(payload.get("message")).strip()
            if not text:
                continue
            phase = str(payload.get("phase", "")).lower()
            kind = "assistant" if phase == "final_answer" or (not phase and looks_like_final_assistant_message(text)) else "thinking"
            label = "Codex" if kind == "assistant" else "Thinking"
            messages.append({"kind": kind, "label": label, "text": text})
            continue

        if top_type == "event_msg" and payload_type == "agent_reasoning":
            text = flatten_text(payload.get("text")).strip()
            if text:
                messages.append({"kind": "thinking", "label": "Thinking", "text": text})
            continue

        if top_type == "response_item" and payload_type == "message":
            role = str(payload.get("role", "")).lower()
            phase = str(payload.get("phase", "")).lower()
            text = flatten_text(payload.get("content")).strip()
            if not text or should_skip_instruction_text(text):
                continue
            if role == "assistant" and phase == "final_answer":
                messages.append({"kind": "assistant", "label": "Codex", "text": text})
            continue

        if top_type == "response_item" and payload_type == "function_call":
            name = str(payload.get("name", "tool"))

cmd: python3 - <<'PY'
import json
from pathlib import Path
for path in Path('/home/ubuntu/.codex/sessions').rglob('*.jsonl'):
    with path.open(encoding='utf-8') as f:
        first = f.readline()
    try:
        obj=json.loads(first)
    except Exception:
        continue
    cwd=obj.get('payload',{}).get('cwd')
    if cwd and ('codex-sessions' in cwd or 'curling' in cwd or 'spechess' in cwd):
        print(cwd, path)

         type=Path,
         help="Output HTML path (defaults to <input>.html)",

+    parser.add_argument(

+        type=Path,

+    parser.add_argument(
+        "--sessions-dir",
+        type=Path,
+        default=Path.home() / ".codex" / "sessions",
+        help="Codex sessions directory to search when input is omitted",
+    )

+        const=Path("."),
+        type=Path,
+        help="Copy the matched rollout file into this directory (or the provided directory) before rendering",
+    )
     return parser.parse_args()
+
+
+def resolve_path(path: Path) -> Path:
+    return path.expanduser().resolve()
+
+
+def read_session_cwd(path: Path) -> str | None:
+    try:
+        with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as file:
+            first_line = file.readline()
+        record = json.loads(first_line)
+    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
+        return None
+
+    payload = record.get("payload")
+    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
+        return None
+    cwd = payload.get("cwd")
+    return cwd if isinstance(cwd, str) and cwd else None
+
+
+def find_rollouts_for_cwd(target_cwd: Path, sessions_dir: Path) -> list[Path]:
+    target = str(resolve_path(target_cwd))
+    root = resolve_path(sessions_dir)
+    if not root.exists():
+        return []
+
+    matches: list[Path] = []
+    for path in root.rglob("rollout-*.jsonl"):
+        session_cwd = read_session_cwd(path)
+        if session_cwd and str(resolve_path(Path(session_cwd))) == target:
+            matches.append(path)
+    return sorted(matches, key=lambda item: item.name)
+
+
+def default_output_path(source: Path, input_was_auto: bool) -> Path:
+    if input_was_auto:
+        return Path.cwd() / f"{source.stem}.html"
+    return source.with_suffix(source.suffix + ".html")

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any


TEXT_KEYS = (
    "text",
    "content",
    "message",
    "output",
    "value",
    "body",
    "stdout",
    "stderr",
    "input",
)


def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Render a Codex rollout/session file as a transcript-style HTML page."
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "input",
        nargs="?",
        type=Path,
        help="Path to the rollout/session file. Omit to use the newest local Codex session for the current directory.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "-o",
        "--output",
        type=Path,
        help="Output HTML path (defaults to <input>.html)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--title",
        help="Optional page title override",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--cwd",
        type=Path,
        help="Directory to match when auto-finding a rollout (defaults to the current working directory)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--sessions-dir",
        type=Path,
        default=Path.home() / ".codex" / "sessions",
        help="Codex sessions directory to search when input is omitted",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--list-matches",
        action="store_true",
        help="List matching local rollout files for the selected cwd and exit",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--copy-rollout",
        nargs="?",
        const=Path("."),
        type=Path,
        help="Copy the matched rollout file into this directory (or the provided directory) before rendering",
    )
    return parser.parse_args()


def resolve_path(path: Path) -> Path:
    return path.expanduser().resolve()


def read_session_cwd(path: Path) -> str | None:
    try:
        with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as file:
            first_line = file.readline()
        record = json.loads(first_line)
    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        return None

    payload = record.get("payload")
Codex
Yes, it is possible here. I updated [rollout_to_html.py](/var/www/html/dev/codex-sessions/rollout_to_html.py) so the rollout input is now optional. If you run it without an input file, it scans `~/.codex/sessions/**/rollout-*.jsonl`, reads each file’s first `session_meta` record, matches `payload.cwd` against the current directory, and renders the newest matching session.

Usage:

```bash
python3 rollout_to_html.py -o transcript.html
```

For another working directory:

```bash
python3 rollout_to_html.py --cwd /var/www/html/dev/spechess -o spechess.html
```

To just see matching rollout files:

```bash
python3 rollout_to_html.py --list-matches
```

To also copy the matched rollout into the current workspace:

```bash
python3 rollout_to_html.py --copy-rollout -o transcript.html
```

I verified it finds the rollout for this workspace and for `/var/www/html/dev/spechess`, and generated [auto-current.html](/var/www/html/dev/codex-sessions/auto-current.html) plus [auto-spechess.html](/var/www/html/dev/codex-sessions/auto-spechess.html). `python3 -m py_compile rollout_to_html.py` passes.

--- task.txt ---
Browser Pitch Tracking Demo — Coding Agent Handoff

Build a small static browser demo that generates a synthetic humming sound with continuously varying pitch and simultaneously estimates that pitch in real time.

The first version should be possible to deploy directly to GitHub Pages with no backend, build server, API keys, microphone access, or external services required.

Core behavior

When the user presses Start, the browser should:

1. Generate a continuous synthetic “hum” using the Web Audio API.
2. Vary its fundamental frequency smoothly and semi-randomly, roughly within 100–400 Hz.
3. Route the generated audio both to the speakers and directly to the pitch-analysis pipeline.
4. Estimate the fundamental frequency from the audio signal in real time.
5. Display both:
   - True/generated pitch
   - Detected pitch
6. Calculate and display pitch error, preferably in cents.
7. Plot true pitch and detected pitch as two scrolling traces over time.

The detector must analyze the generated waveform rather than simply reading the oscillator&#x27;s configured frequency.

Suggested implementation

Keep the first version lightweight:

index.html
app.js
pitch-detector.js
style.css

Use vanilla JavaScript, the Web Audio API, and Canvas or SVG for visualization.

For pitch detection, use a browser-friendly monophonic algorithm such as YIN, McLeod Pitch Method, or a good autocorrelation implementation. Avoid estimating pitch by simply selecting the strongest FFT bin.

Codex
The WPT rollout uses a newer markup shape. User prompts are stored as:

`response_item -> payload.type: message -> role: user`

The parser only handled the older `event_msg -> user_message` form, so `../wpt/wpt.html` had code/output but no user blocks.

I updated [rollout_to_html.py](/var/www/html/dev/codex-sessions/rollout_to_html.py) to handle that newer user-message format, while filtering out environment and instruction records. I also added support for newer `response_item` commentary/reasoning so WPT gets thinking blocks too.

I regenerated [wpt.html](/var/www/html/dev/wpt/wpt.html). It now starts with the user prompt `read task.txt and follow those instructions` and contains 11 user blocks total.
